For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. \"spon_correction_norm\": 76.2, \"interaction_norm\": 147.74, \"num_samples\": 50, \"seq_len\": 2048, \"conclusion_type\": \"weak_subsumption\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:29:47.949Z\"}, (Worker worker_code_ste_baseline_20260515T183635)。修改：src/predictor.py 新增 STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\n\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", \"hypothesis\": \"STE relaxation (hard threshold + straight-through gradient) 是否能匹配 Gumbel-sigmoid 的 KL distillation 效果？如果差距大，说明 Gumbel soft mask 对 KL 训练至关重要（Reviewer Concern #3 defense）。\", |\\n\\n## 重要发现\\nevaluate.py 的 \\\"Dense baseline\\\" 实际是通过 predictor hard mask 的评估（PredictorWrapper._patch_mlps() monkey-patch 所有 MLP，forward_dense() 执行后 sparse_mode 重置为 True）。因此 \\\"dense\\\" PPL neuron。\\n\\n## Per-layer sparsity 分布差异\\n- KL: 非均匀 (0.215-0.806), 中间层低稀疏(保护), 后层高稀疏\\n- BCE: 较均匀 (0.430-0.664), 后层略高\\n\\n## 结论: SUCCESS\\nKL normalized loss 在所有指标上显著优于 BCE: TEAL sparse PPL 12.08 vs {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory\\n\\n## Paper Framing Decisions (Director-approved)\\n\\n### BCE vs training-free: \\\"objective mismatch\\\" 非 \\\"anti-learning\\\" (2026-05-16)\\nBCE (54.84) 比 training-free (23-33) 差。**禁止用 \\\"anti-learning\\\" framing**，改用 **\\\"objective mismatch\\\"**：BCE 优化 per-neuron binary accuracy，TEAL 推理用 global top-k 重分配，两者结构性不兼容。BCE 学到的 per-neuron scoring 在 global budget allocation 下不是最优的。正确 claim: loss function 必须匹配推理管线的 sparsity allocation 机制。KL 直接优化 output distribution，天然兼容任何 allocation 策略。\\n\\n### KL PPL 统一引用规范 (2026-05-16)\\n论文中所有 KL PPL 对比统一用 **4-seed mean 12.00±0.07**（TEAL sparse PPL），不要用单 seed 最佳值 11.91。BCE iso-compute 对比时标注步数（1000 步 vs KL 1000 步，但 BCE per-step FLOPs 仅 KL 的 1/2.71）。\\n\\n### SPON framing: \\\"marginal complement\\\" 非 \\\"subsumption\\\" (2026-05-15)\\n禁止 \\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\" 措辞。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects (cos_sim=0.196, magnitude ratio=16%)。D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing。\\n\\n### s456 outlier 报告方式 (2026-05-16)\\n报 \\\"5-seed mean: 12.32±0.71\\\" + \\\"Excluding s456 (TEAL allocation outlier): 12.00±0.07\\\"。附 per-seed breakdown table + Layer 31 keep ratio 分析。不跑第 6 个 seed。\\n\\n## Key Insights (non-metrics)\\n\\n### KL predictor implicitly subsumes static correction (2026-05-15)\\nSPON bias vectors recover only 3.8% of sparse-vs-dense gap on top of KL predictor (PPL 9.22→9.10). Root cause: KL's end-to-end gradient already captures average-case AND per-input neuron importance through d_logits/dh * dh/dm coupling. Static bias correction is redundant when the contextual predictor is trained with output-distribution alignment.\\n\\n### SPON seq_len=2048 confirms subsumption, rules out mismatch confound (2026-05-15)\\nspon_2048_s42: training seq_len matched eval seq_len=2048. Recovery improved 3.8%→7.3% (+3.5pp), bias norm 0.041→0.053 (+30%), but still < 10% threshold. Even under matched conditions, static bias is fundamentally limited against KL predictor's per-token mask.\\n\\n### Compensation cannot fix BCE's fundamental flaw (2026-05-15)\\nBCE+Compensation (67.2M params, staged training) only improves per-token PPL by 9% (27.96→25.44), still 2.8x worse than KL-only (9.21). TEAL global path completely unaffected by comp network (44.54 = BCE-only). Root cause: BCE gradient dL/ds_j is per-neuron factorized.\\n\\n### Training-free baselines: per-layer normalization is critical (2026-05-16)\\nCRITICAL bug in _global_topk_masks: activation magnitudes vary 50-100x across layers. Fix: divide each layer's importance scores by layer mean before global topk. Corrected PPL: Vanilla TEAL 23.31, WINA 27.93, TEAL Greedy 33.47. R-Sparse still broken (SVD scores need percentile/max normalization). **Same bug likely affects predictor TEAL path** — sparsity70 TEAL PPL=136K anomaly.\\n\\n### BCE objective mismatch with TEAL inference (2026-05-16)\\nBCE iso-compute (1000 steps, reg_weight=2.0, sparsity=0.502) gets TEAL PPL=54.84 — worse than all 3 training-free baselines (23-33). Root cause: BCE optimizes per-neuron binary accuracy independently, but TEAL inference uses global top-k budget allocation across layers. Per-neuron scores optimized for binary accuracy are structurally incompatible with global budget allocation. KL directly optimizes output distribution, naturally compatible with any allocation strategy.\\n\\n### Hard-mask PPL vs TEAL sparse PPL variance separation (2026-05-15)\\n5-seed hard-mask PPL std =
